+# Copyright 2026 Google LLC
+#
+# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
+# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
+# You may obtain a copy of the License at
+#
+#     https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
+#
+# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
+# distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
+# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
+# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
+# limitations under the License.
+
+"""Parameter-selection hook for PEFT-style finetuning of the PyTorch TabFM
+(ttc diagscale fork addition, mirroring the tabpfn fork's finetuning/peft.py).
+
+select_trainable() decides which parameters a finetuning optimizer sees;
+everything else is frozen (requires_grad=False).
+
+Modes:
+    full       all parameters, requires_grad untouched (plain full finetuning)
+    regex      parameters whose fully-qualified name matches cfg["pattern"]
+    diagscale  inject a fresh per-(head,dim) multiplier 1+delta on the query of
+               every MultiheadAttention whose MODULE name matches
+               cfg["pattern"], then train ONLY the deltas. delta is
+               zero-initialized so injection is behavior-preserving (the
+               wrapped model reproduces the pretrained model exactly until
+               training moves delta). The multiplier is applied to the final
+               query right before SDPA (after RoPE / q-RMSNorm /
+               per_dim_scale; SDPA runs at scale=1.0), so it is literally a
+               learned diagonal similarity metric: q̃·k = qᵀ diag(1+δ) k.
+
+Useful patterns (module names of the released TabFM v1.0.0):
+    "."                  all 42 attention modules (53,760 delta params)
+    r"^col_embedder\\."   the FIRST column embedder only — the most upstream
+                         attention that reads the training set (it embeds each
+                         feature's distribution by attending over train rows);
+                         6 modules, 1,536 delta params.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import logging
+import re
+from typing import Any
+
+import torch
+
+from tabfm.src.pytorch.model import MultiheadAttention
+
+logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
+
+
+def inject_diagscale(model: torch.nn.Module, pattern: str) -> int:
+  """Register a zero-init diag_delta [nhead, hd] on every MultiheadAttention
+  whose module name matches `pattern`. In-place; returns the number injected.
+
+  delta is created float32 regardless of the backbone dtype (the forward casts
+  1+delta to the compute dtype), so tiny optimizer updates are not lost to
+  bf16 rounding. No RNG needed (deterministic zero-init).
+  """
+  rx = re.compile(pattern)
+  targets = [(n, m) for n, m in model.named_modules()
+             if isinstance(m, MultiheadAttention) and rx.search(n)]
+  for name, mod in targets:
+    if mod.diag_delta is not None:
+      raise ValueError(f"diagscale already injected at {name!r}")
+    ref = mod.per_dim_scale
+    mod.diag_delta = torch.nn.Parameter(
+        torch.zeros(mod.nhead, mod.hd, device=ref.device, dtype=torch.float32))
+  return len(targets)
+
+
+def select_trainable(
+    model: torch.nn.Module,
+    mode: str = "full",
+    **cfg: Any,
+) -> list[torch.nn.Parameter]:
+  """Freeze/unfreeze `model`'s parameters per `mode`; return the trainables."""
+  named = list(model.named_parameters())
+  n_total = sum(p.numel() for _, p in named)
+
+  if mode == "full":
+    selected = [p for _, p in named]  # requires_grad untouched
+  elif mode == "diagscale":
+    n_injected = inject_diagscale(model, pattern=cfg["pattern"])
+    if n_injected == 0:
+      raise ValueError(
+          f"finetune_mode='diagscale' (cfg={cfg}) matched 0 MultiheadAttention modules")
+    selected = []
+    for name, param in model.named_parameters():  # re-enumerate: deltas now exist
+      train = name.endswith(".diag_delta")
+      param.requires_grad_(train)
+      if train:
+        selected.append(param)
+    if not selected:
+      raise ValueError(
+          f"finetune_mode='diagscale' injected {n_injected} module(s) but selected 0 params")
+    logger.info("finetune_mode=diagscale: injected %d attention module(s)", n_injected)
+  elif mode == "regex":
+    rx = re.compile(cfg["pattern"])
+    selected = []
+    for name, param in named:
+      train = rx.search(name) is not None
+      param.requires_grad_(train)
+      if train:
+        selected.append(param)
+    if not selected:
+      raise ValueError(f"finetune_mode='regex' (cfg={cfg}) selected 0 parameters")
+  else:
+    raise ValueError(f"unknown finetune_mode {mode!r} (full | regex | diagscale)")
+
+  n_selected = sum(p.numel() for p in selected)
+  msg = (f"finetune_mode={mode}: trainable {n_selected:,} / {n_total:,} params "
+         f"({100 * n_selected / n_total:.4f}%)")
+  logger.info(msg)
+  print(f"[tabfm.pytorch.peft] {msg}", flush=True)  # visibility in job logs
+  return selected

new file mode 100644
index 0000000..73f4547
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tabfm/src/pytorch/peft_test.py
@@ -0,0 +1,125 @@
+# Copyright 2026 Google LLC
+#
+# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
+# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
+# You may obtain a copy of the License at
+#
+#     https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
+#
+# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
+# distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
+# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
+# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
+# limitations under the License.
+
+import unittest
+
+import torch
+
+from tabfm.src.pytorch import peft
+from tabfm.src.pytorch.model import MultiheadAttention, TabFM
+
+
+def _tiny_model(seed=0):
+  torch.manual_seed(seed)
+  m = TabFM(
+      embed_dim=16, max_classes=10, col_num_blocks=2, col_nhead=2,
+      col_num_inds=8, row_num_blocks=2, row_nhead=2, row_num_cls=4,
+      icl_num_blocks=2, icl_nhead=2, ff_factor=2, feature_group_size=3,
+      is_classifier=True)
+  # The released checkpoints have non-trivial weights; randomize the zeros-init
+  # params so the identity test is not trivially passing on zero activations.
+  with torch.no_grad():
+    for p in m.parameters():
+      if (p == 0).all():
+        p.normal_(0, 0.02)
+  m.eval()
+  return m
+
+
+def _dummy_batch(seed=0):
+  g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed)
+  x = torch.randn(2, 6, 5, generator=g)
+  y = torch.randint(0, 3, (2, 6), generator=g)
+  train_size = torch.tensor([4, 4])
+  return x, y, train_size
+
+
+class PeftTest(unittest.TestCase):
+
+  def test_injection_is_identity_at_init(self):
+    """Zero-init delta must not change the forward output at all."""
+    m = _tiny_model()
+    x, y, train_size = _dummy_batch()
+    with torch.no_grad():
+      before = m(x, y, train_size)
+    n = peft.inject_diagscale(m, pattern=".")
+    self.assertGreater(n, 0)
+    with torch.no_grad():
+      after = m(x, y, train_size)
+    torch.testing.assert_close(before, after, rtol=0, atol=0)
+
+  def test_select_trainable_diagscale_only_deltas(self):
+    m = _tiny_model()
+    n_attn = sum(1 for _, mod in m.named_modules()
+                 if isinstance(mod, MultiheadAttention))
+    selected = peft.select_trainable(m, mode="diagscale", pattern=".")
+    self.assertEqual(len(selected), n_attn)
+    for name, p in m.named_parameters():
+      self.assertEqual(p.requires_grad, name.endswith(".diag_delta"), name)
+    n_expected = sum(mod.nhead * mod.hd for _, mod in m.named_modules()
+                     if isinstance(mod, MultiheadAttention))
+    self.assertEqual(sum(p.numel() for p in selected), n_expected)
+
+  def test_scoped_pattern_matches_subset(self):
+    m = _tiny_model()
+    n = peft.inject_diagscale(m, pattern=r"^col_embedder\.")
+    # col_embedder: col_num_blocks x (mab1, mab2) = 4 attention modules.
+    self.assertEqual(n, 4)
+    injected = [name for name, _ in m.named_parameters()
+                if name.endswith(".diag_delta")]
+    self.assertEqual(len(injected), 4)
+    for name in injected:
+      self.assertTrue(name.startswith("col_embedder."), name)
+
+  def test_gradient_flows_to_delta_and_moves_output(self):
+    m = _tiny_model()
+    selected = peft.select_trainable(m, mode="diagscale", pattern=".")
+    x, y, train_size = _dummy_batch()
+    out = m(x, y, train_size)
+    loss = out.square().mean()
+    loss.backward()
+    grads = [p.grad for p in selected]
+    self.assertTrue(all(g is not None for g in grads))
+    self.assertTrue(any(g.abs().sum() > 0 for g in grads))
+    # A non-zero delta must change the output (the multiplier is live).
+    with torch.no_grad():
+      before = m(x, y, train_size)
+      for p in selected:
+        p.add_(0.05)
+      after = m(x, y, train_size)
+    self.assertGreater((before - after).abs().max().item(), 0)
+
+  def test_full_mode_untouched(self):
+    m = _tiny_model()
+    selected = peft.select_trainable(m, mode="full")
+    self.assertEqual(len(selected), len(list(m.parameters())))
+    self.assertTrue(all(p.requires_grad for p in m.parameters()))
+
+  def test_double_injection_raises(self):
+    m = _tiny_model()
+    peft.inject_diagscale(m, pattern=".")
+    with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+      peft.inject_diagscale(m, pattern=".")
+
+  def test_state_dict_roundtrip_before_injection(self):
+    """Checkpoint loading (strict) must be unaffected when nothing is injected."""
+    m1, m2 = _tiny_model(seed=1), _tiny_model(seed=2)
+    m2.load_state_dict(m1.state_dict(), strict=True)
+    x, y, train_size = _dummy_batch()
+    with torch.no_grad():
+      torch.testing.assert_close(m1(x, y, train_size), m2(x, y, train_size))
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+  unittest.main()
--

From 78beef73fa74a60808e3ae9a0e90f5d1cad2edf6 Mon Sep 17 00:00:00 2001

Subject: [PATCH 3/3] =?UTF-8?q?feat(peft):=20(IA)3=20baseline=20mode=20?=

MIME-Version: 1.0
Content-Type: text/plain; charset=UTF-8
Content-Transfer-Encoding: 8bit

Liu et al. 2022 adapters as a mechanism ablation ladder against
diagscale: parts={k,v} adds value rescaling to the (logit-equivalent)
diagonal metric; parts={k,v,ff} adds the FFN activation scale. Same
zero-init-delta convention (identity at start, wd pulls to pretrained)
and post-norm placement so lr/wd sweeps are directly comparable.

@@ -71,6 +76,47 @@ def inject_diagscale(model: torch.nn.Module, pattern: str) -> int:
   return len(targets)
 
 
+def inject_ia3(model: torch.nn.Module, pattern: str, parts: tuple = ("k", "v", "ff")) -> int:
+  """(IA)3 (Liu et al. 2022) adapters as zero-init deltas: 1+delta multipliers on keys /
+  values ([nhead, hd], applied post key_ln — the metric-exact analog of diag_delta on q)
+  and/or the FFN intermediate activation ([dim_ff]). `pattern` matches module names;
+  `parts` selects which of k / v / ff to inject. Returns the number of injected tensors.
+
+  Zero-init deltas (rather than the paper's ones-init vectors) keep the multiplier
+  identity at start AND give AdamW weight decay the correct prior (pull toward the
+  pretrained model, not toward a zero function) — consistent with diagscale, so lr/wd
+  sweeps are directly comparable across the two adapter families.
+  """
+  rx = re.compile(pattern)
+  n = 0
+  if "k" in parts or "v" in parts:
+    for name, mod in model.named_modules():
+      if isinstance(mod, MultiheadAttention) and rx.search(name):
+        ref = mod.per_dim_scale
+        if "k" in parts:
+          if mod.ia3_delta_k is not None:
+            raise ValueError(f"ia3 (k) already injected at {name!r}")
+          mod.ia3_delta_k = torch.nn.Parameter(
+              torch.zeros(mod.nhead, mod.hd, device=ref.device, dtype=torch.float32))
+          n += 1
+        if "v" in parts:
+          if mod.ia3_delta_v is not None:
+            raise ValueError(f"ia3 (v) already injected at {name!r}")
+          mod.ia3_delta_v = torch.nn.Parameter(
+              torch.zeros(mod.nhead, mod.hd, device=ref.device, dtype=torch.float32))
+          n += 1
+  if "ff" in parts:
+    for name, mod in model.named_modules():
+      if isinstance(mod, MultiheadAttentionBlock) and rx.search(name):
+        if mod.ia3_delta_ff is not None:
+          raise ValueError(f"ia3 (ff) already injected at {name!r}")
+        ref = mod.linear2.weight
+        mod.ia3_delta_ff = torch.nn.Parameter(
+            torch.zeros(mod.linear2.in_features, device=ref.device, dtype=torch.float32))
+        n += 1
+  return n
+
+

+  def test_ia3_identity_and_selection(self):

+    # k + v per attention, ff per block (blocks == attentions in this model: every
+    # MultiheadAttentionBlock owns exactly one MultiheadAttention).
+    self.assertEqual(len(selected), 3 * n_attn)
+    for name, p in m.named_parameters():
+      is_ia3 = name.rsplit(".", 1)[-1] in ("ia3_delta_k", "ia3_delta_v", "ia3_delta_ff")
+      self.assertEqual(p.requires_grad, is_ia3, name)

+  def test_ia3_parts_subset_and_gradients(self):
+    m = _tiny_model()

+    names = [n for n, p in m.named_parameters() if p.requires_grad]
+    self.assertTrue(all(n.endswith((".ia3_delta_k", ".ia3_delta_v")) for n in names))
+    self.assertFalse(any(n.endswith(".ia3_delta_ff") for n in names))
+    x, y, train_size = _dummy_batch()
+    out = m(x, y, train_size)
+    out.square().mean().backward()
+    self.assertTrue(any(p.grad is not None and p.grad.abs().sum() > 0 for p in selected))

FILE: patches/tabicl/0001-feat-peft-diagscale-IA-3-adapters-for-TabICL-ttc-for.patch

From ba61f05032583195f578d3951e61788a29d2b602 Mon Sep 17 00:00:00 2001
From: Kanghui Ning <>
Date: Wed, 8 Jul 2026 22:54:35 +0000
Subject: [PATCH 1/3] feat(peft): diagscale + (IA)3 adapters for TabICL (ttc
 fork)

- MultiheadAttention: diag_delta / ia3_delta_k / ia3_delta_v slots, plain None
  attrs so released checkpoints still load strict=True
- diag_delta applied to the final query inside sdpa_with_flattened_batch,
  after ssmax (qassmax's query_mlp must see unmodified q) -> logit is exactly
  q^T diag(1+delta) k; verified bit-exact identity at delta=0 on the released
  v2 classifier checkpoint
- MultiheadAttentionBlock: ia3_delta_ff on the FFN intermediate activation
- _model/peft.py: inject_diagscale / inject_ia3 / select_trainable
  (full | regex | diagscale | ia3), ported from the tabfm diagscale fork
- tests/test_peft.py: 10 tests (identity, scopes, counts, grad flow,
  state_dict roundtrip); upstream suite unaffected (126 passed)

diff --git a/src/tabicl/_model/peft.py b/src/tabicl/_model/peft.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..298e98c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/tabicl/_model/peft.py
@@ -0,0 +1,166 @@
+"""Parameter-selection hooks for PEFT-style finetuning of TabICL
+(ttc diagscale fork addition, mirroring the tabfm fork's pytorch/peft.py).
+
+select_trainable() decides which parameters a finetuning optimizer sees;
+everything else is frozen (requires_grad=False).
+
+Modes:
+    full       all parameters, requires_grad untouched (plain full finetuning)
+    regex      parameters whose fully-qualified name matches cfg["pattern"]
+    diagscale  inject a fresh per-(head, dim) multiplier 1+delta on the query of
+               every MultiheadAttention whose MODULE name matches
+               cfg["pattern"], then train ONLY the deltas. delta is
+               zero-initialized so injection is behavior-preserving (the model
+               reproduces the pretrained model exactly until training moves
+               delta). The multiplier is applied to the final query right
+               before SDPA — after RoPE and any SSMax scaling — so the
+               attention logit is exactly a learned diagonal reweighting of
+               the similarity bilinear form: q̃·k = qᵀ diag(1+δ) k.
+    ia3        (IA)3 (Liu et al. 2022) baseline: 1+delta multipliers on the
+               final keys / values and/or the FFN intermediate activation,
+               cfg["parts"] ⊆ {"k","v","ff"} (default all three). Same
+               zero-init delta convention as diagscale so lr/wd sweeps are
+               comparable; the k part is logit-equivalent to diagscale's
+               q-side delta.
+
+Useful patterns (module names of the released TabICL v2 checkpoints):
+    "."                    all 21 attention modules
+    r"^col_embedder\\."     the column embedder's set transformer (6 modules) —
+                           the most upstream attention that reads train rows
+    r"^icl_predictor\\."    the 12 ICL blocks attending over train rows
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import logging
+import re
+from typing import Any
+
+import torch
+
+from .layers import MultiheadAttention, MultiheadAttentionBlock
+
+logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
+
+
+def inject_diagscale(model: torch.nn.Module, pattern: str) -> int:
+    """Register a zero-init diag_delta (num_heads, 1, head_dim) on every
+    MultiheadAttention whose module name matches ``pattern``. In-place;
+    returns the number of modules injected.
+
+    delta is created float32 regardless of the backbone dtype (the forward
+    casts 1+delta to the compute dtype), so tiny optimizer updates are not
+    lost to low-precision rounding. No RNG needed (deterministic zero-init).
+    """
+    rx = re.compile(pattern)
+    targets = [(n, m) for n, m in model.named_modules() if isinstance(m, MultiheadAttention) and rx.search(n)]
+    for name, mod in targets:
+        if mod.diag_delta is not None:
+            raise ValueError(f"diagscale already injected at {name!r}")
+        mod.diag_delta = torch.nn.Parameter(
+            torch.zeros(mod.num_heads, 1, mod.head_dim, device=mod.in_proj_weight.device, dtype=torch.float32)
+        )
+    return len(targets)
+
+
+def inject_ia3(model: torch.nn.Module, pattern: str, parts: tuple = ("k", "v", "ff")) -> int:
+    """(IA)3 (Liu et al. 2022) adapters as zero-init deltas: 1+delta multipliers
+    on the final keys / values ((num_heads, 1, head_dim)) and/or the FFN
+    intermediate activation ((dim_feedforward,)). ``pattern`` matches module
+    names; ``parts`` selects which of k / v / ff to inject. Returns the number
+    of injected tensors.
+
+    Zero-init deltas (rather than the paper's ones-init vectors) keep the
+    multiplier identity at start AND give AdamW weight decay the correct prior
+    (pull toward the pretrained model, not toward a zero function) —
+    consistent with diagscale, so lr/wd sweeps are directly comparable across
+    the two adapter families.
+    """
+    rx = re.compile(pattern)
+    n = 0
+    if "k" in parts or "v" in parts:
+        for name, mod in model.named_modules():
+            if isinstance(mod, MultiheadAttention) and rx.search(name):
+                shape = (mod.num_heads, 1, mod.head_dim)
+                device = mod.in_proj_weight.device
+                if "k" in parts:
+                    if mod.ia3_delta_k is not None:
+                        raise ValueError(f"ia3 (k) already injected at {name!r}")
+                    mod.ia3_delta_k = torch.nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(*shape, device=device, dtype=torch.float32))
+                    n += 1
+                if "v" in parts:
+                    if mod.ia3_delta_v is not None:
+                        raise ValueError(f"ia3 (v) already injected at {name!r}")
+                    mod.ia3_delta_v = torch.nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(*shape, device=device, dtype=torch.float32))
+                    n += 1
+    if "ff" in parts:
+        for name, mod in model.named_modules():
+            if isinstance(mod, MultiheadAttentionBlock) and rx.search(name):
+                if mod.ia3_delta_ff is not None:
+                    raise ValueError(f"ia3 (ff) already injected at {name!r}")
+                mod.ia3_delta_ff = torch.nn.Parameter(
+                    torch.zeros(mod.linear2.in_features, device=mod.linear2.weight.device, dtype=torch.float32)
+                )
+                n += 1
+    return n
+
+
+def select_trainable(
+    model: torch.nn.Module,
+    mode: str = "full",
+    **cfg: Any,
+) -> list[torch.nn.Parameter]:
+    """Freeze/unfreeze ``model``'s parameters per ``mode``; return the trainables."""
+    named = list(model.named_parameters())
+    n_total = sum(p.numel() for _, p in named)
+
+    if mode == "full":
+        selected = [p for _, p in named]  # requires_grad untouched
+    elif mode == "diagscale":
+        n_injected = inject_diagscale(model, pattern=cfg["pattern"])
+        if n_injected == 0:
+            raise ValueError(f"finetune_mode='diagscale' (cfg={cfg}) matched 0 MultiheadAttention modules")
+        selected = []
+        for name, param in model.named_parameters():  # re-enumerate: deltas now exist
+            train = name.endswith(".diag_delta")
+            param.requires_grad_(train)
+            if train:
+                selected.append(param)
+        if not selected:
+            raise ValueError(f"finetune_mode='diagscale' injected {n_injected} module(s) but selected 0 params")
+        logger.info("finetune_mode=diagscale: injected %d attention module(s)", n_injected)
+    elif mode == "ia3":
+        parts = tuple(cfg.get("parts", ("k", "v", "ff")))
+        n_injected = inject_ia3(model, pattern=cfg.get("pattern", "."), parts=parts)
+        if n_injected == 0:
+            raise ValueError(f"finetune_mode='ia3' (cfg={cfg}) matched 0 modules")
+        selected = []
+        for name, param in model.named_parameters():  # re-enumerate: deltas now exist
+            train = any(name.endswith(f".ia3_delta_{p}") for p in ("k", "v", "ff"))
+            param.requires_grad_(train)
+            if train:
+                selected.append(param)
+        if not selected:
+            raise ValueError(f"finetune_mode='ia3' injected {n_injected} tensor(s) but selected 0 params")
+        logger.info("finetune_mode=ia3: injected %d tensor(s) (parts=%s)", n_injected, parts)
+    elif mode == "regex":
+        rx = re.compile(cfg["pattern"])
+        selected = []
+        for name, param in named:
+            train = rx.search(name) is not None
+            param.requires_grad_(train)
+            if train:
+                selected.append(param)
+        if not selected:
+            raise ValueError(f"finetune_mode='regex' (cfg={cfg}) selected 0 parameters")
+    else:
+        raise ValueError(f"unknown finetune_mode {mode!r} (full | regex | diagscale | ia3)")
+
+    n_selected = sum(p.numel() for p in selected)
+    msg = (
+        f"finetune_mode={mode}: trainable {n_selected:,} / {n_total:,} params "
+        f"({100 * n_selected / n_total:.4f}%)"
+    )
+    logger.info(msg)
+    print(f"[tabicl.peft] {msg}", flush=True)  # visibility in job logs
+    return selected
diff --git a/tests/test_peft.py b/tests/test_peft.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..dfd9012
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_peft.py
@@ -0,0 +1,153 @@
+"""Tests for the diagscale / (IA)3 PEFT adapters (ttc fork addition).
+
+The load-bearing property is identity-at-injection: a zero-init delta must
+leave the pretrained model's outputs bit-exactly unchanged, on the same
+forward path the finetuner uses (including qassmax ssmax layers).
+"""
+
+import pytest
+import torch
+
+from tabicl._model.tabicl import TabICL
+from tabicl._model.layers import MultiheadAttention, MultiheadAttentionBlock
+from tabicl._model.peft import inject_diagscale, inject_ia3, select_trainable
+
+
+def make_model(**kwargs) -> TabICL:
+    torch.manual_seed(0)
+    defaults = dict(
+        max_classes=4,
+        embed_dim=32,
+        col_num_blocks=1,
+        col_nhead=2,
+        col_num_inds=8,
+        row_num_blocks=1,
+        row_nhead=2,
+        icl_num_blocks=2,
+        icl_nhead=2,
+        # random (not zeroed) out_proj/linear2 so adapters influence outputs
+        zero_init=False,
+    )
+    defaults.update(kwargs)
+    return TabICL(**defaults).eval()
+
+
+def make_batch(n_rows=24, n_features=5, train_size=16, n_classes=3):
+    g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(1)
+    X = torch.randn(1, n_rows, n_features, generator=g)
+    y_train = torch.randint(0, n_classes, (1, train_size), generator=g).float()
+    return X, y_train
+
+
+def forward(model, X, y_train):
+    with torch.no_grad():
+        return model(X, y_train)
+
+
+def n_attention_modules(model):
+    return sum(1 for _, m in model.named_modules() if isinstance(m, MultiheadAttention))
+
+
+def test_diagscale_identity_at_init():
+    model = make_model()
+    X, y_train = make_batch()
+    before = forward(model, X, y_train)
+    n = inject_diagscale(model, ".")
+    assert n == n_attention_modules(model)
+    after = forward(model, X, y_train)
+    assert torch.equal(before, after)
+
+
+def test_ia3_identity_at_init():
+    model = make_model()
+    X, y_train = make_batch()
+    before = forward(model, X, y_train)
+    inject_ia3(model, ".", parts=("k", "v", "ff"))
+    after = forward(model, X, y_train)
+    assert torch.equal(before, after)
+
+
+def test_nonzero_delta_changes_output():
+    model = make_model()
+    X, y_train = make_batch()
+    before = forward(model, X, y_train)
+    inject_diagscale(model, ".")
+    with torch.no_grad():
+        for _, m in model.named_modules():
+            if isinstance(m, MultiheadAttention):
+                m.diag_delta.fill_(0.3)
+    after = forward(model, X, y_train)
+    assert not torch.allclose(before, after)
+
+
+def test_injection_counts_and_scopes():
+    model = make_model()
+    # col: 2 attentions per ISAB block, row: 1 per block, icl: 1 per block
+    assert n_attention_modules(model) == 2 * 1 + 1 + 2
+    assert inject_diagscale(model, r"^icl_predictor\.") == 2
+    assert inject_diagscale(model, r"^col_embedder\.") == 2
+    assert inject_diagscale(model, r"^row_interactor\.") == 1
+
+
+def test_double_injection_raises():
+    model = make_model()
+    inject_diagscale(model, r"^icl_predictor\.")
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="already injected"):
+        inject_diagscale(model, ".")
+
+
+def test_select_trainable_diagscale():
+    model = make_model()
+    selected = select_trainable(model, mode="diagscale", pattern=".")
+    expected = sum(m.embed_dim for _, m in model.named_modules() if isinstance(m, MultiheadAttention))
+    assert sum(p.numel() for p in selected) == expected
+    for name, p in model.named_parameters():
+        assert p.requires_grad == name.endswith(".diag_delta")
+
+
+def test_select_trainable_ia3_parts():
+    model = make_model()
+    n_attn = n_attention_modules(model)
+    n_blocks = sum(1 for _, m in model.named_modules() if isinstance(m, MultiheadAttentionBlock))
+    n = inject_ia3(model, ".", parts=("k", "ff"))
+    assert n == n_attn + n_blocks
+    ks = [p for name, p in model.named_parameters() if name.endswith(".ia3_delta_k")]
+    vs = [p for name, p in model.named_parameters() if name.endswith(".ia3_delta_v")]
+    ffs = [p for name, p in model.named_parameters() if name.endswith(".ia3_delta_ff")]
+    assert len(ks) == n_attn and len(vs) == 0 and len(ffs) == n_blocks
+
+
+def test_gradient_flow_to_delta_only():
+    model = make_model()
+    select_trainable(model, mode="diagscale", pattern=".")
+    model.train()
+    X, y_train = make_batch()
+    out = model(X, y_train)
+    out.sum().backward()
+    grads = [p.grad for name, p in model.named_parameters() if name.endswith(".diag_delta")]
+    assert all(g is not None for g in grads)
+    assert any(g.abs().sum() > 0 for g in grads)
+    frozen = [p.grad for name, p in model.named_parameters() if not name.endswith(".diag_delta")]
+    assert all(g is None for g in frozen)
+
+
+def test_state_dict_roundtrip():
+    model_a = make_model()
+    inject_diagscale(model_a, ".")
+    with torch.no_grad():
+        for _, m in model_a.named_modules():
+            if isinstance(m, MultiheadAttention):
+                m.diag_delta.fill_(0.1)
+    model_b = make_model()
+    inject_diagscale(model_b, ".")
+    model_b.load_state_dict(model_a.state_dict(), strict=True)
+    X, y_train = make_batch()
+    assert torch.equal(forward(model_a, X, y_train), forward(model_b, X, y_train))
+
+
+def test_pretrained_state_dict_loads_without_adapters():
+    # adapter slots must not appear in state_dict before injection, so
+    # released checkpoints keep loading with strict=True
+    model = make_model()
+    assert not any("delta" in k for k in model.state_dict())
+    model.load_state_dict(make_model().state_dict(), strict=True)
--

Freezing backbone params (requires_grad_(False)) deterministically changes the
GPU train-mode forward through col_embedder by ~5e-3 at delta=0 (flag-keyed
kernel selection; CPU bit-exact; bisected: col_embedder + train-mode only,
inject itself is exact; minimal 4D F.linear repro does NOT reproduce, exact op
not pinned). select_trainable(freeze_others=False) keeps flag parity with the
unfrozen model; callers optimize only the returned params. +1 test (11 passed).

From efda645f3dd620906a55a743d27a9b664299024c Mon Sep 17 00:00:00 2001
From: Kanghui Ning <>
Date: Wed, 15 Jul 2026 02:29:54 +0000
Subject: [PATCH 3/3] fix(peft): KV cache must store pre-rescale K/V;
 select_trainable hardening

- attention.py: need_kv now returns the PRE-(IA)3 K/V for caching; the rescale
  runs on both fresh and cached paths, so caching post-rescale tensors gave
  (1+delta)^2 on every cache reuse (silent, invisible at delta=0).
  New regression test: cached path == fresh path with nonzero deltas
  (verified to fail on the previous code).
- peft.py: mode=full no longer hands requires_grad=False params (rope freqs
  with learned_freq=False) to the optimizer / n_trainable count; mode=regex
  now raises on freeze_others=False instead of silently ignoring it.
---

 src/tabicl/_model/peft.py      |  6 +++++-
 tests/test_peft.py             | 22 ++++++++++++++++++++++
 3 files changed, 32 insertions(+), 4 deletions(-)

+++ b/src/tabicl/_model/attention.py
@@ -285,8 +285,10 @@ def multi_head_attention_forward(
         if rope is not None:
             q = rope.rotate_queries_or_keys(q)
 
-    # (IA)3 adapters rescale the final K/V after projection (and RoPE for K),
-    # covering both the fresh-projection and cached-KV paths
+    # (IA)3 adapters rescale the final K/V after projection (and RoPE for K). The rescale runs on
+    # BOTH the fresh-projection and cached-KV paths, so the need_kv return below must hand back the
+    # PRE-rescale tensors — a cache holding rescaled K/V would get (1+delta)^2 on every reuse.
+    k_raw, v_raw = k, v
     if ia3_delta_k is not None:
         k = k * (1.0 + ia3_delta_k).to(k.dtype)
     if ia3_delta_v is not None:
@@ -337,6 +339,6 @@ def multi_head_attention_forward(
     attn_output = F.linear(attn_output, out_proj_weight, out_proj_bias)  # (batch_shape, tgt_len, E)
 
     if need_kv and cached_kv is None:
-        return attn_output, k, v
+        return attn_output, k_raw, v_raw
 
     return attn_output

+def test_ia3_cached_kv_matches_fresh_path():
+    # the KV cache must store PRE-rescale projections: with nonzero ia3 deltas the
+    # cached path must reproduce the fresh path exactly once (not (1+delta)^2)
+    from tabicl._model.kv_cache import KVCacheEntry
+
+    model = make_model()
+    inject_ia3(model, ".", parts=("k", "v"))
+    with torch.no_grad():
+        for _, m in model.named_modules():
+            if isinstance(m, MultiheadAttention):
+                m.ia3_delta_k.fill_(0.3)
+                m.ia3_delta_v.fill_(-0.2)
+    mha = next(m for _, m in model.named_modules() if isinstance(m, MultiheadAttention))
+    g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(2)
+    q = torch.randn(1, 6, mha.embed_dim, generator=g)
+    kv = torch.randn(1, 10, mha.embed_dim, generator=g)
+    with torch.no_grad():
+        fresh, k_cache, v_cache = mha(q, kv, kv, need_kv=True)
+        cached = mha(q, cached_kv=KVCacheEntry(key=k_cache, value=v_cache))
+    assert torch.allclose(fresh, cached, atol=1e-6)
+
+
 def test_state_dict_roundtrip():
     model_a = make_model()
     inject_diagscale(model_a, ".")

diff --git a/src/tabpfn/finetuning/peft.py b/src/tabpfn/finetuning/peft.py
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..9b44a86c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/tabpfn/finetuning/peft.py
@@ -0,0 +1,64 @@
+"""Parameter-selection hook for PEFT-style finetuning (ttc-dev fork addition).
+
+select_trainable() decides which parameters the finetuning optimizer sees; everything else is
+frozen (requires_grad=False). Wired into FinetunedTabPFNBase via the `finetune_mode` /
+`finetune_cfg` constructor arguments.
+
+Modes:
+    full    all parameters, requires_grad untouched (the original behavior — must stay
+            equivalent to upstream; guarded by the validation ladder's identity rungs)
+    bitfit  bias terms only (BitFit, Ben Zaken et al. 2022)
+    regex   parameters whose fully-qualified name matches finetune_cfg["pattern"] — the
+            explicit form of "train only these layers"; inspect names first via
+            `[n for n, _ in estimator.model_.named_parameters()]`
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import logging
+import re
+from typing import Any
+
+import torch
+
+logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
+
+
+def select_trainable(
+    model: torch.nn.Module,
+    mode: str = "full",
+    **cfg: Any,
+) -> list[torch.nn.Parameter]:
+    """Freeze/unfreeze `model`'s parameters per `mode`; return the trainable parameters."""
+    named = list(model.named_parameters())
+    n_total = sum(p.numel() for _, p in named)
+
+    if mode == "full":
+        selected = [p for _, p in named]  # requires_grad untouched: exact upstream behavior
+    else:
+        if mode == "bitfit":
+            def keep(name: str) -> bool:
+                return name.endswith(".bias")
+        elif mode == "regex":
+            pattern = re.compile(cfg["pattern"])
+
+            def keep(name: str) -> bool:
+                return pattern.search(name) is not None
+        else:
+            raise ValueError(f"unknown finetune_mode '{mode}' (full | bitfit | regex)")
+        selected = []
+        for name, param in named:
+            train = keep(name)
+            param.requires_grad_(train)
+            if train:
+                selected.append(param)
+        if not selected:
+            raise ValueError(f"finetune_mode='{mode}' (cfg={cfg}) selected 0 parameters")
+
+    n_selected = sum(p.numel() for p in selected)
+    msg = (
+        f"finetune_mode={mode}: trainable {n_selected:,} / {n_total:,} params "
+        f"({100 * n_selected / n_total:.2f}%)"
+    )
+    logger.info(msg)
+    print(f"[tabpfn.finetuning.peft] {msg}", flush=True)  # ensure visibility in job logs
+    return selected
--

Subject: [PATCH 3/8] Disable auto_scale_n_estimators in the finetuning

FILE: patches/tabpfn/0004-finetuning-fall-back-to-base-weights-when-best_epoch.patch
================================================
From b2cc770da342a9f63346472bc124555f4f9840b4 Mon Sep 17 00:00:00 2001
From: Kanghui Ning <>
Date: Tue, 16 Jun 2026 18:53:58 +0000
Subject: [PATCH 4/8] finetuning: fall back to base weights when best_epoch=-1
 (early_stopping)
MIME-Version: 1.0
Content-Type: text/plain; charset=UTF-8
Content-Transfer-Encoding: 8bit

When no epoch beats the pre-finetuning validation metric (best_epoch_ == -1),
the loop previously shipped the LAST (degraded) epoch's weights: best_model_state
stayed None so the final restore never fired. Seed best_model_state with a pre-FT
(base) CPU snapshot when early_stopping is on, so the existing restore ships base
weights in that case. early_stopping=False is intentionally untouched (keeps the
last-epoch weights — the fixed-epoch cost-performance trajectory relies on this).

Validated end-to-end via predict_proba (the shipped inference clone): forcing
_is_improvement->False gives early_stopping=True -> max|Δproba| vs base = 0
(restored exactly); early_stopping=False -> 0.71 (degraded, control).
---
 src/tabpfn/finetuning/finetuned_base.py | 20 +++++++++++++++++---
 1 file changed, 17 insertions(+), 3 deletions(-)

diff --git a/src/tabpfn/finetuning/finetuned_base.py b/src/tabpfn/finetuning/finetuned_base.py
index 676af1ea..74d1d2ba 100644
--- a/src/tabpfn/finetuning/finetuned_base.py
+++ b/src/tabpfn/finetuning/finetuned_base.py
@@ -762,10 +762,24 @@ class FinetunedTabPFNBase(BaseEstimator, ABC):
         if is_main_process:
             logger.info("--- 🚀 Starting Fine-tuning ---")
         patience_counter = 0
-        best_model_state: dict[str, torch.Tensor] | None = None
+        # best_model_state is seeded with a snapshot of the PRE-FINETUNING (base) weights so that
+        # if no epoch ever improves over the pre-FT validation metric (best_epoch_ stays -1), the
+        # restore below ships the base checkpoint rather than the last (degraded) epoch's weights.
+        # Gated on early_stopping: with early_stopping=False we intentionally keep the last-epoch
+        # weights (e.g. a fixed-epoch cost-performance trajectory), so no seed is taken. The CPU
+        # clone mirrors the per-improvement snapshot below and bounds GPU memory.
+        best_model_state: dict[str, torch.Tensor] | None = (
+            {
+                k: v.detach().cpu().clone()
+                for k, v in self.finetuned_estimator_.model_.state_dict().items()
+            }
+            if self.early_stopping
+            else None
+        )
         # epoch bookkeeping (ttc-dev): exposed for experiment logging after fit.
-        # best_epoch_ = -1 means no epoch ever improved over the pre-finetuning validation
-        # (note: in that case the final weights are the LAST epoch's, not the pre-FT ones).
+        # best_epoch_ = -1 means no epoch ever improved over the pre-finetuning validation; with
+        # early_stopping on, the restore below then ships the pre-FT (base) weights, so the model
+        # is never worse than base on its own validation split.
         self.epochs_run_ = 0
         self.best_epoch_ = -1
         self.stopped_early_ = False

FILE: patches/tabpfn/0005-finetuning-add-LoRA-mode-low-rank-adapters-to-the-PE.patch
================================================
From 111319e6cae4babeb475407bffa6f5d0d6e5d0ea Mon Sep 17 00:00:00 2001
From: Kanghui Ning <>
Date: Tue, 16 Jun 2026 19:42:25 +0000
Subject: [PATCH 5/8] finetuning: add LoRA mode (low-rank adapters) to the PEFT
 hook
MIME-Version: 1.0
Content-Type: text/plain; charset=UTF-8
Content-Transfer-Encoding: 8bit

New finetune_mode='lora': inject_lora() wraps every nn.Linear whose MODULE name matches
finetune_cfg['pattern'] (default '.*' = full-model LoRA) with a LoRALinear — frozen base
plus a trainable low-rank update B@A*(alpha/r), B zero-initialized so injection is a no-op
at start. select_trainable freezes the base and returns only the adapter params.

B=0 init means a never-improved fit (best_epoch=-1) restored to its init weights reproduces
base exactly, consistent with the base fall-back. Validated on H100: full-LoRA r=8 wraps 313
Linear modules (3.22% trainable); 5 epochs shift predict_proba by 0.22; forced no-improvement
under early stopping restores base predictions exactly (Δ=0).
---
 src/tabpfn/finetuning/peft.py | 67 ++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++-
 1 file changed, 66 insertions(+), 1 deletion(-)

diff --git a/src/tabpfn/finetuning/peft.py b/src/tabpfn/finetuning/peft.py
index 9b44a86c..2b5ae0c6 100644
--- a/src/tabpfn/finetuning/peft.py
+++ b/src/tabpfn/finetuning/peft.py
@@ -11,18 +11,66 @@ Modes:
     regex   parameters whose fully-qualified name matches finetune_cfg["pattern"] — the
             explicit form of "train only these layers"; inspect names first via
             `[n for n, _ in estimator.model_.named_parameters()]`
+    lora    inject low-rank adapters (Hu et al. 2021) into the nn.Linear MODULES whose name
+            matches finetune_cfg["pattern"], freeze the base weights, train only the adapters.
+            finetune_cfg: {pattern, r=8, alpha=16, dropout=0.0}. NB pattern matches MODULE names
+            (e.g. "...attention.q_proj"), not parameter names — inspect via
+            `[n for n, m in estimator.model_.named_modules() if isinstance(m, torch.nn.Linear)]`.
 """
 from __future__ import annotations
 
 import logging
+import math
 import re
 from typing import Any
 
 import torch
+import torch.nn.functional as F
 
 logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
 
 
+class LoRALinear(torch.nn.Module):
+    """Wrap an nn.Linear with a frozen base + a trainable low-rank update B@A·(alpha/r).
+
+    B is zero-initialized so the adapter starts as a no-op (forward == base): injection is
+    therefore behavior-preserving, which also means a never-improved fit (best_epoch=-1) restored
+    to these init weights reproduces base exactly — consistent with the base fall-back.
+    """
+
+    def __init__(self, base: torch.nn.Linear, r: int, alpha: float, dropout: float):
+        super().__init__()
+        self.base = base
+        self.base.weight.requires_grad_(False)
+        if self.base.bias is not None:
+            self.base.bias.requires_grad_(False)
+        self.r = int(r)
+        self.scaling = float(alpha) / float(r)
+        w = base.weight
+        self.lora_A = torch.nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.r, base.in_features, device=w.device, dtype=w.dtype))
+        self.lora_B = torch.nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(base.out_features, self.r, device=w.device, dtype=w.dtype))
+        torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
+        self.lora_dropout = torch.nn.Dropout(dropout) if dropout and dropout > 0 else torch.nn.Identity()
+
+    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:  # base(x) + (x A^T) B^T · scaling
+        return self.base(x) + F.linear(F.linear(self.lora_dropout(x), self.lora_A), self.lora_B) * self.scaling
+
+
+def inject_lora(model: torch.nn.Module, pattern: str, r: int, alpha: float, dropout: float) -> int:
+    """Replace every nn.Linear whose module name matches `pattern` with a LoRALinear. In-place;
+    returns the number of layers wrapped."""
+    rx = re.compile(pattern)
+    targets = [
+        (n, m) for n, m in model.named_modules()
+        if isinstance(m, torch.nn.Linear) and rx.search(n)
+    ]
+    for name, lin in targets:
+        parent_path, _, attr = name.rpartition(".")
+        parent = model.get_submodule(parent_path) if parent_path else model
+        setattr(parent, attr, LoRALinear(lin, r=r, alpha=alpha, dropout=dropout))
+    return len(targets)
+
+
 def select_trainable(
     model: torch.nn.Module,
     mode: str = "full",
@@ -34,6 +82,23 @@ def select_trainable(
 
     if mode == "full":
         selected = [p for _, p in named]  # requires_grad untouched: exact upstream behavior
+    elif mode == "lora":
+        n_wrapped = inject_lora(
+            model,
+            pattern=cfg["pattern"],
+            r=int(cfg.get("r", 8)),
+            alpha=float(cfg.get("alpha", 16)),
+            dropout=float(cfg.get("dropout", 0.0)),
+        )
+        if n_wrapped == 0:
+            raise ValueError(f"finetune_mode='lora' (cfg={cfg}) matched 0 nn.Linear modules")
+        selected = []
+        for name, param in model.named_parameters():  # re-enumerate: adapters now exist
+            train = name.endswith(".lora_A") or name.endswith(".lora_B")
+            param.requires_grad_(train)
+            if train:
+                selected.append(param)
+        logger.info("finetune_mode=lora: wrapped %d Linear module(s)", n_wrapped)
     else:
         if mode == "bitfit":
             def keep(name: str) -> bool:
@@ -44,7 +109,7 @@ def select_trainable(
             def keep(name: str) -> bool:
                 return pattern.search(name) is not None
         else:
-            raise ValueError(f"unknown finetune_mode '{mode}' (full | bitfit | regex)")
+            raise ValueError(f"unknown finetune_mode '{mode}' (full | bitfit | regex | lora)")
         selected = []
         for name, param in named:
             train = keep(name)

From d2674aab2c5b0c73e9bc3817bd22f64057e54920 Mon Sep 17 00:00:00 2001
From: Kanghui Ning <>
Date: Tue, 16 Jun 2026 20:43:23 +0000
Subject: [PATCH 6/8] finetuning: make LoRA adapter init reproducible + guard
 rails
MIME-Version: 1.0
Content-Type: text/plain; charset=UTF-8
Content-Transfer-Encoding: 8bit

inject_lora now seeds the adapter init (kaiming on lora_A) to finetune_cfg['seed']
inside a fork_rng, so it is deterministic given the fit's random_state WITHOUT perturbing
the global RNG the training loop relies on. Previously lora_A drew from the un-seeded global
RNG (injection runs at finetuned_base.py:709, before infer_random_state at :757), so an
identical random_state produced different adapters across runs (non-reproducible LoRA).

Also: LoRALinear rejects r<=0 (was a ZeroDivisionError on alpha/r); the lora branch raises if
it wraps Linears but selects 0 adapter params (parity with the regex/bitfit empty-select guard).

Validated on H100: two same-seed LoRA fits now give bit-identical predict_proba (Δ=0; was
~0.25); LoRA still learns (Δ 0.25 vs base) and the best_epoch=-1 base fall-back holds (Δ=0).

FILE: patches/tabpfn/0007-finetuning-add-diagscale-mode-bare-learnable-diagona.patch
================================================
From d00a2df4a05f1f18f9883c720baee19939d48768 Mon Sep 17 00:00:00 2001
From: Kanghui Ning <>
Date: Tue, 7 Jul 2026 18:13:04 +0000
Subject: [PATCH 7/8] finetuning: add diagscale mode (bare learnable diagonal
 metric on the query)
MIME-Version: 1.0
Content-Type: text/plain; charset=UTF-8
Content-Transfer-Encoding: 8bit

New DiagScale wrapper + inject_diagscale, mirroring the LoRA path: freeze a
SoftmaxScalingMLP's base and multiply its output query by a trainable,
zero-init per-(head,dim) factor, q̃ = base(q,n) * (1+delta). select_trainable
gains a "diagscale" mode that injects the wrapper into modules matching
cfg["pattern"] and trains only .delta. Lets a probe learn the diagonal
similarity metric directly (delta is the readable metric correction), without
finetuning base_mlp or its ln-n conditioning.
---
 src/tabpfn/finetuning/peft.py | 52 ++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++-
 1 file changed, 51 insertions(+), 1 deletion(-)

diff --git a/src/tabpfn/finetuning/peft.py b/src/tabpfn/finetuning/peft.py
index 3b71db87..8be9a10c 100644
--- a/src/tabpfn/finetuning/peft.py
+++ b/src/tabpfn/finetuning/peft.py
@@ -85,6 +85,43 @@ def inject_lora(
     return len(targets)
 
 
+class DiagScale(torch.nn.Module):
+    """Wrap a SoftmaxScalingMLP with a frozen base + a trainable per-(head,dim) multiplier 1+delta.
+
+    delta is zero-initialized so injection starts as a no-op (forward == base). This is the direct,
+    minimal parameterization of the "diagonal similarity metric" hypothesis: rather than finetuning
+    base_mlp, learn a bare constant scale on the query, q̃ = base(q,n) * (1+delta). delta [H,D] is then
+    literally the learned metric correction (readable without reconstructing s from base_mlp weights).
+    Mirrors LoRALinear: frozen base, zero-init adapter, wrapped in place at select_trainable time
+    (after the checkpoint load), so it never interferes with strict loading.
+    """
+
+    def __init__(self, base: torch.nn.Module):
+        super().__init__()
+        self.base = base
+        for p in self.base.parameters():
+            p.requires_grad_(False)
+        h, d = int(base.num_heads), int(base.head_dim)
+        ref = next(self.base.parameters())
+        self.delta = torch.nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(h, d, device=ref.device, dtype=ref.dtype))
+
+    def forward(self, q: torch.Tensor, n: int) -> torch.Tensor:  # base(q,n) * (1+delta), [B,S,H,D]*[H,D]
+        return self.base(q, n) * (1.0 + self.delta)
+
+
+def inject_diagscale(model: torch.nn.Module, pattern: str) -> int:
+    """Replace every SoftmaxScalingMLP whose MODULE name matches `pattern` with a DiagScale wrapper.
+    In-place; returns the number wrapped. No RNG needed (delta is deterministic zero-init)."""
+    rx = re.compile(pattern)
+    targets = [(n, m) for n, m in model.named_modules()
+               if type(m).__name__ == "SoftmaxScalingMLP" and rx.search(n)]
+    for name, mod in targets:
+        parent_path, _, attr = name.rpartition(".")
+        parent = model.get_submodule(parent_path) if parent_path else model
+        setattr(parent, attr, DiagScale(mod))
+    return len(targets)
+
+
 def select_trainable(
     model: torch.nn.Module,
     mode: str = "full",
@@ -116,6 +153,19 @@ def select_trainable(
         if not selected:  # wrapped Linears but no adapter params — should never happen, guard anyway
             raise ValueError(f"finetune_mode='lora' wrapped {n_wrapped} module(s) but selected 0 params")
         logger.info("finetune_mode=lora: wrapped %d Linear module(s)", n_wrapped)
+    elif mode == "diagscale":
+        n_wrapped = inject_diagscale(model, pattern=cfg["pattern"])
+        if n_wrapped == 0:
+            raise ValueError(f"finetune_mode='diagscale' (cfg={cfg}) matched 0 SoftmaxScalingMLP modules")
+        selected = []
+        for name, param in model.named_parameters():  # re-enumerate: delta params now exist
+            train = name.endswith(".delta")
+            param.requires_grad_(train)
+            if train:
+                selected.append(param)
+        if not selected:
+            raise ValueError(f"finetune_mode='diagscale' wrapped {n_wrapped} module(s) but selected 0 params")
+        logger.info("finetune_mode=diagscale: wrapped %d SoftmaxScalingMLP module(s)", n_wrapped)
     else:
         if mode == "bitfit":
             def keep(name: str) -> bool:
@@ -126,7 +176,7 @@ def select_trainable(
             def keep(name: str) -> bool:
                 return pattern.search(name) is not None
         else:
-            raise ValueError(f"unknown finetune_mode '{mode}' (full | bitfit | regex | lora)")
+            raise ValueError(f"unknown finetune_mode '{mode}' (full | bitfit | regex | lora | diagscale)")
         selected = []
         for name, param in named:
             train = keep(name)

================================================
FILE: scripts/export_hf_results.py

#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Build the Hugging Face dataset folder with the paper's per-cell TabArena results.

Layout written to ``--out`` (nothing is uploaded)::

    README.md                         dataset card
    data/results.parquet              all 12 arms in one table (12 x 816 rows)
    data/arms.parquet                 one row per arm: config, Elo, Delta Elo, GPU hours, sha256, ...
    data/arms.csv                     the same table as CSV, for reading without a parquet reader
    raw/<arm>/results_per_split.csv   the arm's own TabArena-format rows, byte-identical lines

For every paper arm only our own rows (the non-reference ``Demo_*`` method) of the run's
``results_per_split.csv`` are copied, line by line, so the Elo fit on ``raw/`` stays bit-identical.
The frozen TabFM arm (``raw/tabfm_frozen``) is also the 68th entry of the fixed reference field.
The 67 official TabArena reference entries are NOT redistributed: ``ttc.eval.elo`` fetches them
from TabArena. Both dataset configs are parquet because ``datasets.load_dataset`` infers one builder
for all configs of a repository (a csv config next to a parquet one fails to load).

    python scripts/export_hf_results.py --runs-dir $TTC_RUNS_DIR/eval \
        --paper-arms docs/paper/artifacts/elo68_paper/paper_arms.csv \
        --points docs/paper/artifacts/headline_cost/points.csv --out hf_export/

Arms are taken from ``points.csv`` (backbone, method, run_tag, Elo, GPU hours; 12 rows) joined with
the normalized score of ``paper_arms.csv``; ``--tags`` restricts the export to a subset of run tags
(the frozen TabFM reference always ships).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import hashlib
import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path

_SRC = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "src"
if (_SRC / "ttc" / "eval" / "elo.py").exists():
    sys.path.insert(0, str(_SRC))

import pandas as pd  # noqa: E402

from ttc.eval.elo import CANDIDATE_PREFIX, FROZEN_TABFM_TAG, N_CELLS, REFERENCE_METHODS  # noqa: E402

GITHUB = "https://github.com/kanghui-learning/test-time-compute-for-tabular-foundation-models"
HF_REPO = "nkh/test-time-compute-for-tabular-foundation-models-results"

BACKBONES = {"TabPFN-3": "tabpfn3", "TabICL v2": "tabiclv2", "TabFM": "tabfm"}
# points.csv method label -> (release config name, method label in the dataset, learning rate)
METHODS = {
    "Frozen": ("frozen", "frozen"),
    "32 views": ("native_views_32", "native views (32)"),
    "Full FT": ("full_ft", "full fine-tuning"),
    "DiagScale": ("diagscale", "DiagScale"),
    "96-config. aggregation": ("aggregation_96", "aggregation (96 configurations)"),
    "DiagScale + aggregation": ("diagscale_aggregation_96", "DiagScale + aggregation"),
}
# Learning rate of each adaptation arm (= ft_lr of configs/<backbone>/<method>.yaml).
LEARNING_RATES = {
    "tabpfn3_full_ft": 1e-6, "tabpfn3_diagscale": 3e-2, "tabpfn3_diagscale_aggregation_96": 1e-2,
    "tabiclv2_full_ft": 1e-5, "tabiclv2_diagscale": 3e-2,
    "tabfm_full_ft": 1e-4, "tabfm_diagscale": 1e-2,
}
ARM_ORDER = [
    "tabpfn3_frozen", "tabpfn3_native_views_32", "tabpfn3_full_ft", "tabpfn3_diagscale",
    "tabpfn3_aggregation_96", "tabpfn3_diagscale_aggregation_96",
    "tabiclv2_frozen", "tabiclv2_full_ft", "tabiclv2_diagscale",
    "tabfm_frozen", "tabfm_full_ft", "tabfm_diagscale",
]
# Textual TabArena columns that are empty for our rows; kept as strings (null), not float NaN.
STRING_COLS = ["dataset", "tabarena_method", "metric", "problem_type", "impute_method", "method_type",
               "method_subtype", "config_type", "ta_name", "ta_suite"]
LEADING_COLS = ["dataset", "fold", "tabarena_method", "metric", "metric_error", "metric_error_val",
                "time_train_s", "time_infer_s"]


def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def load_arms(paper_arms: Path, points: Path) -> pd.DataFrame:
    pts = pd.read_csv(points)
    scores = pd.read_csv(paper_arms).set_index(["backbone", "setting"])["score"]
    rows = []
    for p in pts.itertuples():
        cfg_method, method = METHODS[p.method]
        arm = f"{BACKBONES[p.backbone]}_{cfg_method}"
        rows.append(dict(
            arm=arm, backbone=p.backbone, method=method, learning_rate=LEARNING_RATES.get(arm),
            config=f"configs/{BACKBONES[p.backbone]}/{cfg_method}.yaml",
            elo=p.elo_raw, delta_elo=p.delta_elo_raw, delta_elo_rounded=int(p.delta_elo),
            baseline=p.baseline, normalized_score=scores[(p.backbone, p.method)],
            gpu_hours=p.total_h, source_run=p.run_tag))
    arms = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    assert set(arms.arm) == set(ARM_ORDER), sorted(set(arms.arm) ^ set(ARM_ORDER))
    return arms.set_index("arm").loc[ARM_ORDER].reset_index()


def copy_candidate_rows(src: Path, dst: Path) -> tuple[str, int, int]:
    """Copy header + non-reference rows verbatim; returns (method, n_cells, n_datasets)."""
    dst.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    methods, cells = set(), set()
    with open(src, newline="") as f_in, open(dst, "w", newline="") as f_out:
        header = f_in.readline()
        f_out.write(header)
        cols = next(csv.reader([header]))
        i_m, i_d, i_f = cols.index("method"), cols.index("dataset"), cols.index("fold")
        for line in f_in:
            row = next(csv.reader([line]))
            if row[i_m] in REFERENCE_METHODS:
                continue
            methods.add(row[i_m])
            cells.add((row[i_d], row[i_f]))
            f_out.write(line)
    if len(methods) != 1 or not next(iter(methods)).startswith(CANDIDATE_PREFIX):
        raise ValueError(f"{src}: expected one {CANDIDATE_PREFIX}* method, got {sorted(methods)}")
    if len(cells) != N_CELLS:
        raise ValueError(f"{src}: expected {N_CELLS} cells, got {len(cells)}")
    return methods.pop(), len(cells), len({d for d, _ in cells})


def read_raw(path: Path) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Read a raw results_per_split.csv with exact float parsing and the dataset's column names."""
    df = pd.read_csv(path, float_precision="round_trip").rename(columns={"method": "tabarena_method"})
    for col in df.columns:
        if col in STRING_COLS:
            df[col] = df[col].astype("string")
    df["fold"] = df["fold"].astype("int64")
    return df[LEADING_COLS + [c for c in df.columns if c not in LEADING_COLS]]


def results_table(arms: pd.DataFrame, out: Path) -> pd.DataFrame:
    parts = []
    for a in arms.itertuples():
        df = read_raw(out / a.raw_file)
        df.insert(0, "learning_rate", a.learning_rate)
        df.insert(0, "method", a.method)
        df.insert(0, "backbone", a.backbone)
        df.insert(0, "arm", a.arm)
        parts.append(df)
    res = pd.concat(parts, ignore_index=True)
    for col in ("arm", "backbone", "method"):
        res[col] = res[col].astype("string")
    res["learning_rate"] = res["learning_rate"].astype("float64")
    return res.sort_values(["arm", "dataset", "fold"], kind="stable").reset_index(drop=True)


def fmt_lr(lr) -> str:
    return "" if pd.isna(lr) else f"{lr:.0e}".replace("e-0", "e-")


CARD = """---
license: cc-by-4.0
pretty_name: Test-Time Compute for Tabular Foundation Models - TabArena Results
task_categories:
- tabular-classification
- tabular-regression
tags:
- tabular
- benchmark
- tabarena
- test-time-compute
- tabular-foundation-models
- tabpfn
- tabicl
size_categories:
- 1K<n<10K
configs:
- config_name: results
  data_files: data/results.parquet
  default: true
- config_name: arms
  data_files: data/arms.parquet
---

# Test-Time Compute for Tabular Foundation Models: TabArena Results

Per-cell TabArena test errors for the 12 methods ("arms") in the main comparison of
*Test-Time Compute for Tabular Foundation Models: Mechanisms, Gains, and Limits*.
Every arm covers all 51 TabArena datasets (816 cells), so these files are enough to recompute
the paper's Elo ratings and paired comparisons.

**Code:** [GitHub]({github}) · **Paper:** [arXiv:2610.12005](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12005)

## Files

| file | content |
|---|---|
| `data/results.parquet` | config `results`: one row per arm × cell ({n_rows:,} rows) |
| `data/arms.parquet`, `data/arms.csv` | config `arms`: one row per arm (config, Elo, ΔElo, GPU hours, file checksum) |
| `raw/<arm>/results_per_split.csv` | each arm in TabArena's format, the input to `ttc.eval.elo` |

## Arms

ΔElo is measured against TabArena's default entry for TabPFN-3 and TabICL v2, and against our
frozen run for TabFM. GPU hours are fit + predict time over 816 cells on one H100.

{arms_table}

## Columns of `results`

| column | meaning |
|---|---|
| `arm`, `backbone`, `method`, `learning_rate` | arm identity; `learning_rate` is null without fine-tuning |
| `dataset`, `fold` | TabArena dataset and split (`fold` enumerates repeat × fold) |
| `metric`, `metric_error` | `roc_auc` (binary, error = 1 − AUC), `log_loss` (multiclass) or `rmse` (regression); lower is better |
| `time_train_s`, `time_infer_s` | wall-clock seconds per cell |
| `problem_type` | `binary`, `multiclass` or `regression` |
| other columns | TabArena bookkeeping (`tabarena_method`, `imputed`, ...); `metric_error_val` is not recorded (null) |

## Usage

```python
from datasets import load_dataset

repo = "{hf_repo}"
results = load_dataset(repo, "results", split="train")
arms = load_dataset(repo, "arms", split="train")
```

Recompute an Elo rating with the code repository:

```bash
hf download {hf_repo} \\
    --repo-type dataset --local-dir hf_results
python -m ttc.eval.elo \\
    --candidate hf_results/raw/tabpfn3_diagscale/results_per_split.csv \\
    --reference-field hf_results/raw/tabfm_frozen/results_per_split.csv \\
    --baseline "TA-TABPFN-3 (default)"            # ΔElo +26
```

## Notes

- **Elo.** Fixed field of 68 entries: the 67 official TabArena entries (fetched by TabArena, not
  included here) plus our frozen TabFM run. Each arm is added alone and rated with TabArena's
  Bradley–Terry solver, `RF (default)` = 1000.
- GPU fine-tuning is not bitwise deterministic; re-running an adaptation config gives slightly
  different cell errors.

## License

The results are released under CC-BY-4.0. TabArena data and reference results, and the TabPFN-3,
TabICL v2 and TabFM weights (none included), are under their own licenses.

## Citation

```bibtex
@article{{ning2026testtime,
  title   = {{Test-Time Compute for Tabular Foundation Models: Mechanisms, Gains, and Limits}},
  author  = {{Ning, Kanghui and Bilo{{\\v{{s}}}}, Marin and Wilson, James T. and Zhang, Yilang and Rasul, Kashif
             and Song, Dongjin and Schneider, Anderson and Nevmyvaka, Yuriy}},
  journal = {{arXiv preprint arXiv:2610.12005}},
  year    = {{2026}}
}}
```
"""


def arms_markdown(arms: pd.DataFrame) -> str:
    lines = ["| backbone | arm | LR | Elo | ΔElo | GPU h |", "|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|"]
    prev = None
    for a in arms.itertuples():
        backbone = "" if a.backbone == prev else a.backbone
        prev = a.backbone
        delta = f"{a.delta_elo_rounded:+d}" if a.delta_elo_rounded else "0"
        lines.append(f"| {backbone} | [`{a.arm}`]({GITHUB}/blob/main/{a.config}) | {fmt_lr(a.learning_rate)} "
                     f"| {a.elo:.0f} | {delta} | {a.gpu_hours:.1f} |")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.split("\n\n")[0])
    runs_default = os.environ.get("TTC_RUNS_DIR")
    ap.add_argument("--runs-dir", type=Path, default=Path(runs_default) / "eval" if runs_default else None,
                    help="folder of <tag>/results_per_split.csv (default $TTC_RUNS_DIR/eval)")
    ap.add_argument("--paper-arms", type=Path, required=True, help="artifacts/elo68_paper/paper_arms.csv")
    ap.add_argument("--points", type=Path, required=True, help="artifacts/headline_cost/points.csv")
    ap.add_argument("--tags", nargs="*", help="export only these run tags (default: all 12 arms)")
    ap.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)
    if args.runs_dir is None:
        ap.error("--runs-dir (or $TTC_RUNS_DIR) is required")

    arms = load_arms(args.paper_arms, args.points)
    if args.tags:
        keep = arms.source_run.isin(args.tags) | arms.source_run.map(lambda t: Path(t).name).isin(args.tags)
        arms = arms[keep | (arms.source_run == FROZEN_TABFM_TAG)].reset_index(drop=True)

    extra = []
    for a in arms.itertuples():
        src = args.runs_dir / a.source_run / "results_per_split.csv"
        raw_file = Path("raw") / a.arm / "results_per_split.csv"
        dst = args.out / raw_file
        _, n_cells, n_ds = copy_candidate_rows(src, dst)
        extra.append(dict(n_cells=n_cells, n_datasets=n_ds, raw_file=str(raw_file), sha256=sha256(dst)))
        print(f"{a.arm:36s} {a.source_run:60s} {dst.stat().st_size / 1e3:8.1f} kB")
    arms = pd.concat([arms, pd.DataFrame(extra)], axis=1)
    arms = arms[["arm", "backbone", "method", "learning_rate", "config", "elo", "delta_elo",
                 "delta_elo_rounded", "baseline", "normalized_score", "gpu_hours", "n_cells",
                 "n_datasets", "raw_file", "sha256", "source_run"]]

    (args.out / "data").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    res = results_table(arms, args.out)
    res.to_parquet(args.out / "data" / "results.parquet", index=False)
    arms.to_parquet(args.out / "data" / "arms.parquet", index=False)
    arms.to_csv(args.out / "data" / "arms.csv", index=False)
    card = CARD.format(github=GITHUB, hf_repo=HF_REPO, n_rows=len(res), arms_table=arms_markdown(arms))
    (args.out / "README.md").write_text(card)
    total = sum(p.stat().st_size for p in args.out.rglob("*") if p.is_file())
    print(f"{len(arms)} arms, {len(res)} rows -> {args.out}  total {total / 1e6:.2f} MB")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())



================================================
FILE: scripts/install_forks.sh
================================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Install the three patched backbones (tabpfn, tabicl, tabfm) and the pinned TabArena
# benchmark packages into the CURRENT Python environment.
#
# For each backbone this script:
#   1. clones the public upstream repository into $THIRD_PARTY_DIR/<pkg>,
#   2. checks out the exact upstream base commit the paper used,
#   3. applies our patch series from patches/<pkg>/ with `git am` (authorship kept),
#   4. verifies the resulting source tree is bit-identical to the tree we ran
#      (git tree-hash check), and
#   5. runs `pip install -e`.
# TabArena (autogluon/tabarena) is checked out at a pinned commit and its two
# subpackages (packages/bencheval, packages/tabarena) are installed editable, unpatched.
#
# Usage:
#   bash scripts/install_forks.sh [--dir DIR] [--only "tabpfn tabicl tabfm tabarena"] [--no-pip]
#   THIRD_PARTY_DIR=/path PIP="python -m pip" bash scripts/install_forks.sh
#
# Idempotent: re-running on an already-installed checkout verifies the tree hash and
# re-runs `pip install -e` (cheap). A checkout that is dirty or on an unexpected tree
# is NOT modified; the script stops with an error so you can inspect it.
set -euo pipefail

REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
PATCH_DIR="$REPO_ROOT/patches"
THIRD_PARTY_DIR="${THIRD_PARTY_DIR:-$REPO_ROOT/third_party}"
PIP="${PIP:-python -m pip}"
ONLY="tabpfn tabicl tabfm tabarena"
DO_PIP=1

while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
    case "$1" in
        --dir) THIRD_PARTY_DIR="$2"; shift 2 ;;
        --only) ONLY="$2"; shift 2 ;;
        --no-pip) DO_PIP=0; shift ;;
        -h|--help) sed -n '2,24p' "$0"; exit 0 ;;
        *) echo "ERROR: unknown argument: $1" >&2; exit 2 ;;
    esac
done

die() { echo "ERROR: $*" >&2; exit 1; }
log() { echo "[install_forks] $*"; }

command -v git >/dev/null || die "git not found"
mkdir -p "$THIRD_PARTY_DIR"
THIRD_PARTY_DIR="$(cd "$THIRD_PARTY_DIR" && pwd)"

# name | upstream URL | base commit | expected tree after patch ("-" = no patch) | pip targets
#   The expected tree hash is the git tree of the exact fork commit used for the paper:
#     tabpfn  fork commit bf9b2c99590bdfe9f32bdd8ede77095ec5c5bb2c
#     tabicl  fork commit efda645f3dd620906a55a743d27a9b664299024c
#     tabfm   fork commit 78beef73fa74a60808e3ae9a0e90f5d1cad2edf6
SPECS=(
    "tabpfn|https://github.com/PriorLabs/TabPFN.git|95ff6772152eadc43e97b2a9d27899d44551eba8|f2094982e7858328c1cf7f7c39b9d8a3f8f0de88|."
    "tabicl|https://github.com/soda-inria/tabicl.git|46b91961db4f8873dd049ec09990698a435e1e29|2df5bffc050befbd0c77dae34f5aa4fab11d08be|."
    "tabfm|https://github.com/google-research/tabfm.git|2fb67e86477e0d2d3466baed00c2fc43053b0973|7abb81574e911a38affe36a4ce4902fd223218e9|.[pytorch]"
    "tabarena|https://github.com/autogluon/tabarena.git|abd24c7f06e4294e86efe069fe1cd057293a17d1|-|packages/bencheval packages/tabarena"
)

# Committer identity for `git am` (authorship comes from the patches themselves).
# Fixed dates make the resulting commit hashes reproducible across machines.
GIT_AM=(git -c user.name="tfm-ttc-release" -c user.email="tfm-ttc-release@localhost"
        -c core.autocrlf=false am --committer-date-is-author-date --keep-cr)

head_tree() { git -C "$1" rev-parse 'HEAD^{tree}'; }

prepare_checkout() {
    local name="$1" url="$2" base="$3" want_tree="$4"
    local dir="$THIRD_PARTY_DIR/$name"

    if [ -d "$dir/.git" ]; then
        log "$name: existing checkout at $dir"
        [ -z "$(git -C "$dir" status --porcelain --untracked-files=no)" ] \
            || die "$name: $dir has uncommitted changes; refusing to touch it"
        git -C "$dir" cat-file -e "$base^{commit}" 2>/dev/null \
            || git -C "$dir" fetch --quiet origin "$base" \
            || die "$name: base commit $base not available in $dir"
    elif [ -e "$dir" ]; then
        die "$name: $dir exists but is not a git checkout"
    else
        log "$name: cloning $url"
        git clone --quiet --filter=blob:none --no-checkout "$url" "$dir" \
            || die "$name: clone failed"
        git -C "$dir" cat-file -e "$base^{commit}" 2>/dev/null \
            || git -C "$dir" fetch --quiet origin "$base" \
            || die "$name: base commit $base not found upstream"
    fi

    if [ "$want_tree" = "-" ]; then
        # Unpatched package: just sit on the base commit.
        if [ "$(git -C "$dir" rev-parse HEAD 2>/dev/null || true)" != "$base" ]; then
            git -C "$dir" checkout --quiet --detach "$base" || die "$name: checkout $base failed"
        fi
        [ "$(git -C "$dir" rev-parse HEAD)" = "$base" ] || die "$name: HEAD is not $base"
        log "$name: at $base (no patch)"
        return
    fi

    # Already patched? (tree identical to the paper's fork commit)
    if git -C "$dir" rev-parse --verify --quiet HEAD >/dev/null \
        && [ "$(head_tree "$dir")" = "$want_tree" ]; then
        log "$name: already patched (tree $want_tree)"
        return
    fi

    local series=("$PATCH_DIR/$name"/*.patch)
    [ -e "${series[0]}" ] || die "$name: no patches found in $PATCH_DIR/$name"

    log "$name: checking out base $base and applying ${#series[@]} patches"
    git -C "$dir" checkout --quiet -B ttc-release "$base" || die "$name: checkout $base failed"
    (cd "$dir" && "${GIT_AM[@]}" --quiet "${series[@]}") || {
        git -C "$dir" am --abort >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
        die "$name: git am failed (patches do not apply to $base)"
    }
    local got
    got="$(head_tree "$dir")"
    [ "$got" = "$want_tree" ] \
        || die "$name: patched tree $got != expected $want_tree (source differs from the paper's fork)"
    log "$name: patched OK (tree $got)"
}

pip_install() {
    local name="$1" targets="$2"
    local dir="$THIRD_PARTY_DIR/$name" t args=()
    for t in $targets; do
        if [ "$t" = "." ] || [[ "$t" == .\[* ]]; then
            args+=(-e "$dir${t#.}")
        else
            args+=(-e "$dir/$t")
        fi
    done
    log "$name: $PIP install ${args[*]}"
    $PIP install "${args[@]}" || die "$name: pip install failed"
}

for spec in "${SPECS[@]}"; do
    IFS='|' read -r name url base want_tree targets <<<"$spec"
    case " $ONLY " in *" $name "*) ;; *) continue ;; esac
    prepare_checkout "$name" "$url" "$base" "$want_tree"
    [ "$DO_PIP" = 1 ] && pip_install "$name" "$targets"
done

log "done (third-party dir: $THIRD_PARTY_DIR)"

"""ttc: test-time compute for tabular foundation models.

    run.py            YAML config -> one TabArena run
    registry.py       config model keys -> model classes
    benchmarks/       TabArena runner; large-table row retrieval
    models.py         per-task finetuning (full / DiagScale) for TabPFN-3, TabICL v2 and TabFM
    backbones/        TabFM model wrapper and the TabICL/TabFM finetuning loops
    agg_model.py      TabPFN-3 configuration pool + greedy reducer
    agg_ft_model.py   DiagScale adaptation followed by the configuration pool
    aggregation/      configuration search space and reducers
    retrieval/        attention-guided row retrieval helpers
    eval/             Elo against the fixed reference field, paired error changes
"""
from __future__ import annotations

__version__ = "1.0.0"



================================================
FILE: src/ttc/agg_ft_model.py
================================================
"""DiagScale adaptation followed by the configuration pool.

The backbone is adapted once per outer cell, then every configuration in the pool is built from
the adapted backbone and reduced by the same out-of-fold greedy selection as
``AggregationTabPFN3Model``.

The adapter is injected rather than saved as a checkpoint. ``inject_diagscale`` replaces each
``SoftmaxScalingMLP`` with a wrapper that holds the frozen module as ``.base`` and a trainable
``.delta`` (forward: ``base(q, n) * (1 + delta)``), so the adapted state dict cannot be loaded into
a stock TabPFN-3. The trained ``.delta`` tensors are kept in memory and re-injected into each
member after its fit builds the model.

This class overrides two hooks of ``AggregationTabPFN3Model``:
  * ``_fit``              adapts first (``ft_*`` hyperparameters, TabPFN's finetuning loop with its
                          own 90/10 split for early stopping), keeps the deltas, then calls
                          ``super()._fit``;
  * ``_build_estimator``  wraps each member so the deltas are injected after it fits.

Information flow: the adapter is fitted on the outer training split, and the out-of-fold
predictions that choose the reducer weights are computed on that same split. Each inner fold is
held out of the context, but its labels may have influenced the shared adapter, so the selector
inputs are out of fold with respect to the context only. Outer test labels are used only for
evaluation.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import time

from ttc.agg_model import AggregationTabPFN3Model

# Modes this composition supports: they ADD adapter tensors and leave the pretrained weights frozen,
# so a member can load the stock checkpoint and then have the adapters injected. Weight-modifying
# modes (full / bitfit / regex — regex trains the ORIGINAL parameters selected by name) are rejected:
# there is no separable adapter to carry over.
_ADAPTER_MODES = ("diagscale", "lora")
# ...but tabpfn's select_trainable indexes cfg["pattern"] for these (KeyError without it).
_PATTERN_MODES = ("diagscale", "lora", "regex")


class _DeltaInjectedEstimator:
    """Proxy around a TabPFN estimator that re-injects finetuned adapter params after ``fit``.

    TabPFN builds ``model_`` lazily inside ``fit``, so injection cannot happen at construction time.
    Everything except ``fit`` is forwarded to the wrapped estimator (``predict`` / ``predict_proba``
    / ``classes_`` / ...), so this is a drop-in for ``AggregationTabPFN3Model``'s call pattern.
    """

    def __init__(self, est, adapter_sd: dict, pattern: str, mode: str):
        self._est = est
        self._adapter_sd = adapter_sd
        self._pattern = pattern
        self._mode = mode

    def fit(self, X, y):
        self._est.fit(X, y)
        model = getattr(self._est, "model_", None)
        if model is None:
            raise RuntimeError("member estimator has no model_ after fit — cannot inject the delta")
        if self._mode == "diagscale":
            from tabpfn.finetuning.peft import inject_diagscale
            n = inject_diagscale(model, pattern=self._pattern)
            if n == 0:
                raise RuntimeError(f"inject_diagscale matched 0 modules for pattern {self._pattern!r}")
        elif self._mode == "lora":
            from tabpfn.finetuning.peft import inject_lora
            inject_lora(model, pattern=self._pattern)
        # strict=False: adapter_sd holds ONLY the adapter tensors; every pretrained weight is
        # already loaded from the stock checkpoint and must stay as-is.
        missing, unexpected = model.load_state_dict(self._adapter_sd, strict=False)
        if unexpected:
            raise RuntimeError(f"adapter keys not present in the member model: {list(unexpected)[:3]}")
        return self

    def __getattr__(self, name):  # only called when normal lookup fails -> forwards to the estimator
        return getattr(self._est, name)


class FinetunedAggregationTabPFN3Model(AggregationTabPFN3Model):
    """Aggregation member pool over a per-task finetuned TabPFN-3 backbone.

    Hyperparameters: the union of the two parents' —
        ``aggregation: {...}``  the member pool / reduction knobs (see AggregationTabPFN3Model)
        ``ft_*``                the finetuning knobs (ft_epochs / ft_lr / ft_mode / ft_regex ...);
                                ``ft_epochs <= 0`` skips the finetune entirely, which makes this
                                model bit-identical to plain ``AggregationTabPFN3Model``
                                (the identity rung that validates the composition).
    """

    ag_key = "TTC-AGGFT-TABPFN3"
    ag_name = "TTC-AggFT-TabPFN3"

    # ── every member is built from the stock checkpoint, then gets the deltas injected ──
    def _build_estimator(self, native_cfg: dict, n_estimators: int):
        est = super()._build_estimator(native_cfg, n_estimators)
        sd = getattr(self, "_ft_adapter_sd", None)
        if not sd:
            return est                        # no finetune (identity rung) -> plain aggregation
        return _DeltaInjectedEstimator(est, sd, self._ft_pattern, self._ft_mode)

    # ── finetune the backbone once; keep the trained adapter tensors ──
    def _finetune_backbone(self, X, y, num_cpus: int, num_gpus: int, ft: dict) -> dict:
        import torch
        from tabpfn.finetuning import FinetunedTabPFNClassifier, FinetunedTabPFNRegressor

        is_clf = self.problem_type in ("binary", "multiclass")
        device = self._resolve_tabpfn_device(num_gpus=num_gpus)
        if not isinstance(device, str):
            device = device[0]
        extra = {
            "model_path": self._get_model_checkpoint(),
            "categorical_features_indices": self._categorical_indices,
            "n_jobs": num_cpus,
        }
        mode = str(ft.get("ft_mode", "diagscale"))
        # tabpfn's select_trainable does cfg["pattern"] unconditionally for the injecting modes, so
        # they MUST get one even when the config omits ft_regex (default "." = every module).
        pattern = str(ft.get("ft_regex", "."))
        ft_cfg = {"pattern": pattern} if mode in _PATTERN_MODES else None
        common = {
            "device": device,
            "epochs": int(ft.get("ft_epochs", 10)),
            "learning_rate": float(ft.get("ft_lr", 1e-2)),
            "random_state": self.fixed_random_state,
            "time_limit": ft.get("ft_time_limit"),
            "finetune_mode": mode,
            "finetune_cfg": ft_cfg,
        }
        cls = FinetunedTabPFNClassifier if is_clf else FinetunedTabPFNRegressor
        kw = {"extra_classifier_kwargs": extra} if is_clf else {"extra_regressor_kwargs": extra}
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        ftm = cls(**common, **kw).fit(X=X, y=y)
        secs = time.monotonic() - t0

        inner = getattr(ftm, "finetuned_estimator_", None)
        model = getattr(inner, "model_", None) if inner is not None else None
        if model is None:
            raise RuntimeError("finetuning produced no finetuned_estimator_.model_")
        # keep ONLY the adapter tensors (diagscale: '*.delta'; lora: '*.lora_A/B'). The pretrained
        # weights are untouched by these modes, so every member gets them from the stock checkpoint.
        suffixes = (".delta", ".lora_A", ".lora_B")
        sd = {k: v.detach().to("cpu").clone()
              for k, v in model.state_dict().items() if k.endswith(suffixes)}
        if not sd:
            raise RuntimeError(f"no adapter tensors found for ft_mode={mode!r} "
                               "(is it a weight-modifying mode? this path supports adapters only)")
        self._ft_mode, self._ft_pattern = mode, pattern
        self._ft_seconds = round(secs, 2)
        n_par = int(sum(v.numel() for v in sd.values()))
        del ftm, inner, model
        if torch.cuda.is_available():
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
        print(f"[agg-ft] finetuned {mode}@lr{common['learning_rate']:g} {common['epochs']}ep "
              f"n={len(X)} -> {len(sd)} adapter tensors / {n_par:,} params in {secs:.1f}s", flush=True)
        return sd

    def _fit(self, X, y, num_cpus: int = 1, num_gpus: int = 0, **kwargs):
        ft = self._get_ft_params()
        if int(ft.get("ft_epochs", 0)) > 0:
            if str(ft.get("ft_mode", "diagscale")) not in _ADAPTER_MODES:
                raise ValueError(
